\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1)\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}+\|v_{tz}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}\leq C.

(3.43)

This along with (
2.15
), (
3.7
), (
3.40
) and (
3.42
) implies that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{2}}^{2}\leq C.

By analogous arguments as in (
3.43
), we further derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1)\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{2}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon})\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|\langle z\rangle^{l}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{2}}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{-\alpha},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{k}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1)\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}=\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{k}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon})\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\|\langle z\rangle^{l}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{k}}^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{k}\|\langle z\rangle^{l}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1)\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{k+1-j}}^{2}\|v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{j}}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{-3\alpha}

for
k=3,4
. In a similar way, one can derive the estimates on higher-order derivatives of
{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1
. Precisely, we have the following estimates

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1)\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{k}({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-1)\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C

for
5\leq 2i+k\leq 6
with
i\geq 2
. This along with (
2.15
), (
3.7
) and (
3.40
) yields that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2i}\partial_{z}^{k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }2i+k=5,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2i}\partial_{z}^{k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }6\leq 2i+k\leq 7.

As for the time-weighted estimate for
\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}
, notice that the time-weighted estimates on
v^{B,\varepsilon}
is independent of
\varepsilon
, in other words, the time weight functions can remove the dependence of the norms on
\varepsilon
caused by the corner-correctors. Therefore the time-weighted estimate for
\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}
can be derived by similar arguments as proving the
\varepsilon
-dependent estimates. The details are omitted for brevity.
∎

The following lemma states the regularity of
(\varphi^{b,\varepsilon},v^{b,\varepsilon})
which can be proved by a similar procedure as proving Lemma
3.3
.

Lemma 3.4
.

Assume the conditions in Lemma
3.2
hold. Then for any
T>0
, there exists a constant
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{T}>0
such that if
\varepsilon\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}_{T}
, the problem (
2.9
), (
2.10
) admits a unique solution
(v^{b,\varepsilon},\varphi^{b,\varepsilon})
on
[0,T]
which satisfies

\displaystyle\displaystyle 0\leq v^{b,\varepsilon}\leq v_{\ast},\ \ \|v_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\varphi_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\leq C,

(3.44a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{3-2k}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{4-2k}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,

(3.44b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{4-2i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{5-2i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }i=0,1,2,

(3.44c)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C\ \mbox{for }\,2i+k=5,

(3.44d)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{6-2i}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }\,i=0,1,2,

(3.44e)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{6-2i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{7-2i}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }i=0,1,2,3,

(3.44f)

where
C>0
is a constant depending on
T
, but independent of
\varepsilon
.

Similar to Corollary
3.2
, in view of Proposition
A.1
in Appendix A, we have the following delicate estimates for
(\varphi^{b,1},v^{b,\varepsilon})
.

Corollary 3.3
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
3.4
, it holds for any
l\in\mathbb{N}
and
k=0,1
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{2-2k}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{3-2k}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{4-2k}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{5-2k}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.45)

and that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{3-2k}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{4-2k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.46a)

\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{5-2k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.46b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{6-2k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.46c)

where
C>0
is a constant depending on
T
, but independent of
\varepsilon
.

We next turn to the existence and regularity of the outer layer profile
(\varphi^{I,1},v^{I,1})
.

Lemma 3.5
.

Assume the conditions in Lemma
3.2
hold, and let
\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}
and
\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}
be the solution obtained in Lemmas
3.3
and
3.4
, respectively. Then for any
T>0
, the problem (
2.20
) admits a unique classical solution
(\varphi^{I,1},v^{I,1})
on
[0,T]
which satisfies

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{I,1}\in L_{T}^{2}H^{6-2k}\ \mbox{for }k=0,1,2,3,\ \ v^{I,1}\in L_{T}^{\infty}H^{5}\ \ \mbox{and}\ \ \partial_{t}^{k}v^{I,1}\in L_{T}^{2}H^{7-2k}\ \mbox{for}\ k=1,2,3,

along with the bounds

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{i}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{2-2i}}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}\leq C,

(3.47a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1+i}{2}}\partial_{x}^{1+i}\varphi_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{x}^{3-2i}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C,

(3.47b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{2+\frac{i}{2}}\partial_{x}^{1+i}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{t}^{j}\partial_{x}^{5-2j}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\|(t^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{3}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C,

(3.47c)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{3i+2}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha(2i+1)}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2+i}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha(2i+1)}{2}}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{2+2i}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}v_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}\leq C,

(3.47d)

where
i=0,1
,
k=0,1
,
j=0,1,2
,
C>0
is a constant independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

The local existence and uniqueness of solutions to the problem (
2.20
) on the outer layer profile pair
(\varphi^{I,1},v^{I,1})
can be proved by the classical PDE theory for linear parabolic equations (cf.
[
11
, Sec. 7.1]
) along with the Banach fixed point theorem. In the following, we will devote ourselves to establishing some
a priori
estimates which entail the global existence and the desired regularity of the solution.

Denote
b(x,t):=x\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)+(1-x)\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)
and
\tilde{\varphi}:=\varphi^{I,1}+b(x,t)
with

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)=-\int_{0}^{\infty}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)\left({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}(y,t)}-1\right)\mathrm{d}y,\\[11.38109pt]
\displaystyle\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)=\int_{-\infty}^{0}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)+M)\left({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{b,\varepsilon}(y,t)}-1\right)\mathrm{d}y.\end{cases}

Then we deduce from (
2.20
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{t}=\tilde{\varphi}_{xx}-(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}+M)v_{x}^{I,1}-\tilde{\varphi}_{x}v_{x}^{I,0}+f_{1}(x,t),\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v_{t}^{I,1}=-\left(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}+M\right)v^{I,1}-\tilde{\varphi}_{x}v^{I,0}+f_{2}(x,t),\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}(0,t)=\tilde{\varphi}(1,t)=0,\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle(\tilde{\varphi},v^{I,1})(x,0)=(0,0),\end{cases}

(3.48)

where we have used the fact
v^{B,\varepsilon}(z,0)=v^{b,\varepsilon}(\xi,0)=0
, and
f_{i}(x,t)~(i=1,2)
are given by

\displaystyle\displaystyle f_{1}(x,t):=b_{t}+b_{x}v_{x}^{I,0},\ \ f_{2}(x,t):=b_{x}v^{I,0},\ \ k=0,1.

(3.49)

To ensure the desired regularity of the solution, it is necessary to derive some estimates for
f_{1}(x,t)
and
f_{2}(x,t)
. By (
3.10
) and (
A.2
), we deduce for
k=0,1
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\leq C\|\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}\leq C\|\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}\leq C,

(3.50a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{1+3k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2k)\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2+k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\leq\|(t^{\frac{1+3k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2k)\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2+k}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}\leq C.

(3.50b)

Similarly, by (
3.44
) and (
A.2
), we have for
\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\|(t^{\frac{1+3k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2k)\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2+k}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1.

(3.51)

Recalling the definitions of
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
in (
3.49
), we deduce for
k=0,1,2
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}f_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{4-2k}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|\partial_{t}^{k+1}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{k+1}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=0}^{k}\left(\|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad~\quad\quad\times\|\partial_{t}^{k-j}v^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{5-2k}}^{2},

(3.52a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}f_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{5-2k}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{k}\left(\|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad~\quad\times\|\partial_{t}^{k-j}v^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{5-2k}}^{2},

(3.52b)

which along with (
3.7
), (
3.50
) and (
3.51
) implies that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{(2k+1)\alpha}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{1+k}f_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{2-2k}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}f_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\|f_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{4}}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}f_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{5-2k}}\leq C,\ \ k=0,1.

(3.53)

By analogous arguments as deriving (
3.52
), we get, thanks to (
3.7
), (
3.50
) and (
3.51
),

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{3k+1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{1+k}f_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{2-2k}}+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}f_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}\leq C,\ \ k=0,1.

(3.54)

With the temporal weight functions identified in (
3.54
), we are ready to establish estimates for the solution. The remaining proof only involves tedious calculations by using the similar arguments as those in the proof of Lemma
3.3
, and hence we omit the details for brevity.
∎

With the estimates in (
3.47
), one can utilize Proposition
A.1
to get the following
L^{\infty}
estimates.

Corollary 3.4
.

Let
(\varphi^{I,1},v^{I,1})
be the solution obtained in Lemma
3.5
. It holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|t\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{x}^{4}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C,

(3.55a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\varphi_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|t^{2+\frac{i}{2}}\partial_{x}^{1+i}\varphi_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{x}^{2}\varphi_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C,

(3.55b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C,

(3.55c)

and that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}v_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{x}^{4}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C,

(3.56a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{3i+2}{2}}\partial_{t}^{1+i}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2i)\alpha}{2}}\partial_{t}^{1+i}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{2}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{3}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C.

(3.56b)

Here
i=0,1
, the constant
C>0
is independent of
\varepsilon
.

With the estimates for lower-order profiles derived in Lemmas
3.2
–
3.5
at hand, we proceed to derived the estimates for higher-order profiles determined by the systems (
2.21
) and (
2.22
).

Lemma 3.6
.

Assume the conditions in Lemmas
3.2
,
3.3
and
3.5
hold. Then the problem (
2.21
) admits a unique solution
(v^{B,1},\varphi^{B,2})
on
[0,T]
for any
T\in(0,\infty)
which satisfies, for any
l\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{B,1}\in L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{6-2k}\ \ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,2,3,\ \mbox{and }\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{B,2}\in L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{7-2k}\ \ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,2.

Precisely, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{2-2k}}+\|\langle z\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{3+3k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2k)\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2+k}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,\ k=0,1,

(3.57a)

\displaystyle\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{z}^{3-2k}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{z}^{4-2k}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,\ \ k=0,1,

(3.57b)

\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{5+k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{5+k-2i}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,\ i=0,1,2,\ k=0,1

(3.57c)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{3}}+\|(t^{\frac{2+3j}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2j)\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{1+j}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{2-2j}}\leq C,\ \ j=0,1,

(3.57d)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{4}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{4}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{5}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.57e)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{3}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.57f)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{6-2i}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,\ \ i=0,1,2.

(3.57g)

Proof.

The ideas and technicalities of the proof of Lemma
3.6
involves tedious and complicated calculations, the ideas and procedures are essentially similar to those in the proof of Lemma
3.5
. For brevity, we conclude the proof without presenting the details here.
∎

With Lemma
3.6
, the following estimates can be readily derived based on Proposition
A.1
in the Appendix A.

Corollary 3.5
.

Let
(v^{B,1},\varphi^{B,2})
be the solution obtained in Lemma
3.6
.
It holds for
i=0,1
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2i)\alpha}{2}}\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{2+2i}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|t^{\frac{2+i}{2}}\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{2+i}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.58a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5+i}{2}}\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{4+i}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}\leq C,

(3.58b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5+i}{2}}\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{2+i}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}\leq C,

(3.58c)

and that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{4}})\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{2}}+\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{3}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.59a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{4}}))\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{4}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{z}^{5}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.59b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\alpha})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{z}^{3}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.59c)

where
C>0
is a constant independent of
\varepsilon
.

Similar to Lemma
3.6
, we have the following existence and regularity result for
(\varphi^{b,2},v^{b,1})
.

Lemma 3.7
.

Assume the conditions in Lemmas
3.2
,
3.3
and
3.5
hold. Then there exists a unique solution
(\varphi^{b,2},v^{b,1})
to the problem (
2.22
) on
[0,T]
for any
T\in(0,\infty)
such that for any
l\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{b,1}\in L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{6-2k}\ \ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,2,3,\ \mbox{and }\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\varphi^{b,2}\in L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{7-2k}\ \ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,2,

with

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{2-2k}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}(t^{\frac{3+3k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2k)\alpha}{2}})\partial_{t}^{2+k}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,\ \ k=0,1,

(3.60a)

\displaystyle\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{3-2k}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{4-2k}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,\ \ k=0,1,

(3.60b)

\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{5+k}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{5+k-2i}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,\ i=0,1,2,\ \ k=0,1

(3.60c)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{3}}+\|(t^{\frac{2+3j}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2j)\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{1+j}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{2-2j}}\leq C,\ \ j=0,1,

(3.60d)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{4}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{4}})\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{5}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.60e)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{\xi}^{3}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.60f)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{\xi}^{6-2i}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,\ \ i=0,1,2.

(3.60g)

Similar to Corollary
3.5
, we have

Corollary 3.6
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{(1+2i)\alpha}{2}}\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{2+2i}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\|t^{\frac{2+i}{2}}\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{2+i}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.61a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5+i}{2}}\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{4+i}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{\xi}^{2}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\leq C,

(3.61b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5+k}{2}}\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{\xi}^{2+k}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{2}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\leq C,

(3.61c)

and that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{4}})\|\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t+\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{3}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.62a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{4}}))\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{4}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{\xi}^{5}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.62b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|(t^{2}+\varepsilon^{\alpha})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{2}}+\|(t^{3}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}})\langle\xi\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{\xi}^{3}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\leq C,

(3.62c)

where
i=0,1
,
C>0
is a constant independent of
\varepsilon
.

4.
Convergence of boundary layers

This section is devoted to proving Theorem
2.1
, while Theorem
2.2
is a consequence of Theorem
2.1
by the change of variables as briefed in Section 1.

4.1.
Reformulation of the problem

Denote by
(\varphi^{\varepsilon},v^{\epsilon})
the solution to the problem (
2.2
). To study the convergence of boundary-layer profiles stated in Theorem
2.1
, by noticing that
\varphi^{B,0}=\varphi^{B,0}\equiv 0
, we write
(\varphi^{\varepsilon},v^{\epsilon})
as

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\varphi^{\varepsilon}=\varphi^{I,0}(x,t)+\varepsilon^{1/2}(\varphi^{I,1}(x,t)+\varphi^{B,1}(z,t)+\varphi^{b,1}(\xi,t))+\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v^{\varepsilon}=v^{I,0}(x,t)+v^{B,\varepsilon}(z,t)+v^{b,\varepsilon}(\xi,t)+\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\varepsilon},\end{cases}

and derive equations for the remainder
(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon},\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\varepsilon})
to show for any
T>0
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3\iota_{0}}{2}-\frac{3}{8}},\ \ \|t^{\frac{5}{4}}\partial_{x}\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},\ \ \ \|\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}-\frac{1}{4}},

(4.1)

where
1/2<\iota_{0}<7/12
is as in (
2.26
). Note that the estimate (
2.27
) naturally holds for small
\varepsilon>0
under (
4.1
) since
\frac{3\iota_{0}}{2}-\frac{3}{8}<\frac{1}{2}
. However, if we substitute the above perturbations into the equations in (
2.2
), we shall find that the equations of
\mathcal{E}_{i}^{\varepsilon}
have some terms at the zero-th order of
\varepsilon
, which are obstacles to derive the estimates in (
4.1
). To resolve this emerging issue, we approximate the solution of (
2.2
), (
2.2c
) with some higher-order profiles as follows:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Phi^{a}(x,t)

\displaystyle:=\varphi^{I,0}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\varphi^{I,1}(x,t)+\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(z,t)+\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(\xi,t)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\left(\varphi^{B,2}(z,t)+\varphi^{b,2}(\xi,t)\right)+b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}(x,t),

\displaystyle\displaystyle V^{a}(x,t)

\displaystyle:=v^{I,0}(x,t)+v^{B,\varepsilon}(z,t)+v^{b,\varepsilon}(\xi,t)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(v^{I,1}(x,t)+v^{B,1}(z,t)+v^{b,1}(\xi,t)\right)+b_{v}^{\varepsilon}(x,t),

(4.2)

where the functions
b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}(x,t)
and
b_{v}^{\varepsilon}(x,t)
are constructed below to homogenize the boundary values of
(\Phi^{a},V^{a})
:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\makebox[-0.5pt]{~}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}(x,t)

\displaystyle=-(1-x)\left[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)+\varepsilon\varphi^{b,2}(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)\right]-(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\varepsilon\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-x\left[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)+\varepsilon\varphi^{B,2}(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)\right]-x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\varepsilon\varphi^{b,2}(0,t),

(4.3a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\makebox[-0.5pt]{~}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}(x,t)

\displaystyle=(x-1)\left[v^{b,\varepsilon}(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v^{b,1}(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)+\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-x\left[v^{B,\varepsilon}(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v^{B,1}(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t)+\Lambda_{2}^{\varepsilon}(t)\right],

(4.3b)

where
\nu
is as in (
2.25
). Then we perturb
(\varphi^{\varepsilon},v^{\varepsilon})
as

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varphi^{\varepsilon}-\Phi^{a}=\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi^{\varepsilon},\ \ v^{\varepsilon}-V^{a}=\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}V^{\varepsilon}.

(4.4)

Clearly, we have

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}&=\varepsilon^{1/2}\Phi^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon\left(\varphi^{B,2}(z,t)+\varphi^{b,2}(\xi,t)\right)+b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}(x,t),\\[2.84526pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\varepsilon}&=\varepsilon^{1/2}V^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{1/2}(v^{I,1}(x,t)+v^{B,1}(z,t)+v^{b,1}(\xi,t))+b_{v}^{\varepsilon}(x,t).\end{cases}

(4.5)

In view of the estimates in the previous section for the outer- and boundary-layer profiles, to get (
4.1
), it now remains to derive the equations for
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
and establish some desired estimates. Substituting (
4.4
) into (
2.2
), then the perturbation functions
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
satisfy

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}=\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}-\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{a}-V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon},\\[2.84526pt]
\displaystyle V_{t}^{\varepsilon}=\varepsilon V_{xx}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{\varepsilon}-\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{a}-(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)V^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon},\\
\displaystyle(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})(x,0)=(0,0),\\
\displaystyle(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})(0,t)=(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})(1,t)=(0,0),\end{cases}

(4.6)

where the error terms
\mathcal{R}_{i}^{\varepsilon}\,(i=1,2)
are given by

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}=\Phi_{xx}^{a}-(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)V_{x}^{a}-\Phi_{t}^{a},\ \ \ \mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}=\varepsilon V_{xx}^{a}-(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)V^{a}-V_{t}^{a}.

(4.7)

Notice that the coefficients and error terms in the problem (
4.6
) only involve the outer- and boundary-layer profiles which have been studied in the previous section. By standard arguments (cf.
[
30
,
24
]
), one can prove the local existence of solutions to the problem (
4.6
) with
\varepsilon>0
on a time interval
[0,T_{\varepsilon}]
for some
T_{\varepsilon}>0
which may be small. Now the key of the matter is to establish some
a priori
estimates for
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
uniformly in
\varepsilon
from which one can extract the convergence of boundary layers. We also remark that following the procedure of establishing the uniform estimates, one can easily derive some higher-order estimates depending on
\varepsilon
. With these estimates, one can obtain the existence of solutions on the time interval
[0,T]
for any
T>0
for fixed
\varepsilon>0
.. Specifically, we will prove the following results for the problem (
4.6
).

Proposition 4.1
.

Assume the conditions in Theorem
2.1
hold. Then for any
T>0
, there exists a constant
\varepsilon_{T}>0
depending on
T
such that for any
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{T}]
, the problem (
4.6
) admits a unique solution
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})\in C([0,T];H^{2}\times H^{2})
which satisfies for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}-2\iota_{0}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|V^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},

\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xx}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}},

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|V_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}-2\iota_{0}}\|\Phi_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}-2\iota_{0}}(\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},

where
C>0
is a constant depending on
T
but independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

The proof of Proposition
4.1
consists of Lemmas
4.1
–
4.6
in the sequel.
∎

4.2.
A priori
estimates

4.2.1.
Estimates on the error terms

Now let us turn to estimates on the error terms
\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}
and
\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}
. Using (
4.7
) and the first equations in (
2.6
) and in (
2.20
), we get from a direct computation that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}=\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}+\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon},

(4.8)

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=-v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\left[\varphi_{x}^{I,0}-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)-x\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}(0,t)\right]-v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\left[\varphi_{x}^{I,0}-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)-(x-1)\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\left[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v_{x}^{B,1}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t))+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v_{x}^{b,1}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}(v_{x}^{I,0}-v_{x}^{I,0}(0,t))+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}(v_{x}^{I,0}-v_{x}^{I,0}(1,t))\right]-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}+v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon(v_{x}^{B,1}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}+v_{x}^{b,1}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{B,2}v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,2}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon})-\varepsilon\left[\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(v_{x}^{B,1}+v_{x}^{b,1})\right]-\varepsilon\varphi_{x}^{I,1}v_{x}^{I,1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon\varphi_{x}^{B,2}\left[v^{I,0}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1})\right]_{x}-\varepsilon\varphi_{x}^{b,2}\left[v^{I,0}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1})\right]_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\left[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\varphi_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{t}^{b,\varepsilon})+\varepsilon(\varphi_{t}^{B,2}+\varphi_{t}^{b,2})\right]+F^{\varepsilon}=:\sum_{i=1}^{11}\mathcal{P}_{i}+F^{\varepsilon},

(4.9)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=-\left[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(0,t))+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(1,t))\right]-\varepsilon v_{x}^{I,1}(\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon})

(4.10)

with

\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\left(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}+M+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}+\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\right)+\varepsilon\left(\varphi_{x}^{B,2}+\varphi_{x}^{b,2}\right)\right)

(4.11)

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\left(v^{I,0}+v^{B,\varepsilon}+v^{b,\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1}\big)\right)_{x}-\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}-\partial_{t}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle-\partial_{x}^{2}\left[(1-x)\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\varepsilon\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\right]-\partial_{x}^{2}\left[x\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\varepsilon\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\right].

The estimates on
\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}
and
\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}
can be stated as follows.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
0<\varepsilon<1
. It holds for any
T>0
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\makebox[-2.0pt]{~}\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|t\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}},\ \ \|\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},

(4.12)

where
\iota_{0}=\min\left\{\frac{3}{4}-\nu,\frac{\alpha}{2},\frac{2+\nu-\alpha}{2},\frac{2-3\nu}{2}\right\}
, the constant
C>0
may depend on
T
but independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

By Taylor’s formula, (
3.7
), (
3.11
), (
A.2
) and (
A.6a
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}=\left\|\frac{\varphi_{x}^{I,0}-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)-x\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}(0,t)}{x^{2}}x^{2}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{x}^{3}\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|x^{2}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon\|\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{4}}\|z^{2}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{4}}\|z^{2}v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}.

(4.13)

Similarly, by (
3.8
), (
3.11
), (
3.57
), (
A.4
) and (
A.6b
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{x}^{3}\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|(x-1)^{2}v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{3}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|xv_{x}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|(x-1)v_{x}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{4}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|x\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|(x-1)\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}.

Notice that
\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{1/2}}<z=\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{1/2}}<\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}}
for
1/2\leq x\leq 1
, and that
-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}}\leq\xi=\frac{x-1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}}\leq-\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{1/2}}
for
0\leq x\leq 1/2
. This along with (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
3.58
) and (
3.61
) implies for
m\in\mathbb{N}
and
k=0,1,2
that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,\frac{1}{2})\times(0,T))}

\displaystyle~=\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\left(\varepsilon^{-\frac{m}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{m}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,\frac{1}{2})\times(0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\|z^{m+i}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{z}^{i}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L_{z}^{\infty}(0,\varepsilon^{-1/2}))}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\|\xi^{m+i}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L_{\xi}^{\infty}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},0))}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle^{m+i}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{z}^{i}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\|\langle\xi\rangle^{m+i}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{\xi}^{i}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m-3\alpha}{2}},

(4.14)

where
i=0,1,\cdots,4-2k
, and
C
is a positive constant independent of
\varepsilon
. Similarly, we have for
m\in\mathbb{N}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}v^{B,1}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}v^{b,1}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,\frac{1}{2})\times(0,T))}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m-3\alpha}{2}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,1/2)\times(0,T))}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m-3\alpha}{2}}

(4.15)

for
k=0,1,2
,
i=0,1,\cdots,4-2k
, and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{x}^{i}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,1/2)\times(0,T))}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m-3\alpha}{2}},

(4.16)

if
k=0,1
,
i=0,1,\cdots,4-2k
. Therefore, we deduce for
m\in\mathbb{N}\cap\left\{m\geq 1+3\alpha\right\}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}((0,1/2)))}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}((0,1/2)))}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,1/2)\times(0,T))}\|v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\ \left.+\|v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}\|\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{2m+1-6\alpha}{4}}\left(\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{2m+1-6\alpha}{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

(4.17)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}((0,1/2)))}+\varepsilon^{1/2}\|v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}(0,T;L^{2}((1/2,1)))}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,1/2)\times(0,T))}\|\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\ \left.+\|\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((1/2,1)\times(0,T))}\|v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{2m+1-6\alpha}{4}}\left(\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{2m+1-6\alpha}{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

(4.18)

where we have used (
A.6
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{5}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}.

(4.19)

By similar arguments as proving estimates for
\mathcal{P}_{5}
, one can infer that
\|\mathcal{P}_{6}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}
. Thanks to (
3.8
), (
3.47
), (
A.6
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, we derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{7}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{8}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon,

(4.20)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{9}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon\|\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\right\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}\left(\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{10}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{11}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\varphi_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{t}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{t}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

(4.21)

For the last term
F^{\varepsilon}
, by similar arguments as proving (
4.14
), we first deduce from (
4.3a
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{B,2}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{b,2}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2-\nu}{2}},

(4.22)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{B,2}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi^{b,2}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon\|\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-\nu},

(4.23)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi_{t}^{B,2}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{m+1}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}\varphi_{t}^{b,2}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|\varphi_{t}^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\varphi_{t}^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2+\nu-\alpha}{2}},

(4.24)

where
m\in\mathbb{N}\cap\left\{m\geq 1+3\alpha\right\}
is fixed, and we have used the fact
1<\alpha<1+\nu<5/4
. Similarly, given any integer
m\geq 1
, by (
4.3b
), (
3.1
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, we have the following estimate for
b_{v}^{\varepsilon}
:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\left(\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}v^{B,\varepsilon}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\left\|\varepsilon^{\frac{1-m}{2}}v^{B,1}(\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\right.

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad\left.\quad\quad~\ \ \quad\quad+\left\|\varepsilon^{-m/2}v^{b,\varepsilon}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{1-m}{2}}v^{b,1}(-\varepsilon^{-1/2},t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\|\Lambda_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}\left(\|\langle z\rangle^{m}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\langle z\rangle^{m-1}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{m}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\qquad~\qquad\qquad~\qquad\left.+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{m-1}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{m}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

(4.25)

Therefore we get by (
4.22
)–(
4.25
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|F^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\left(1+\left\|\varphi^{I,0}\right\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad~\ \ \quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\ \ \ \qquad\left.+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)+\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\varepsilon(\|\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\|\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))})\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}-\nu}+C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2+\nu-\alpha}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2-3\nu}{2}}.

(4.26)

Summing up, we now have for
0<\varepsilon<1
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{11}\|\mathcal{P}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|F^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}},

(4.27)

where
\iota_{0}
is as in (
2.26
). That is,
\iota_{0}=\min\left\{\frac{3}{4}-\nu,\frac{\alpha}{2},\frac{2+\nu-\alpha}{2},\frac{2-3\nu}{2},\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2}\right\}
. For
\|\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
, we get from (
3.11
), (
3.12
) and (
3.45
)–(
3.47
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{I}}|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}|^{2}\left|\int_{0}^{x}\varphi_{yy}^{I,1}\mathrm{d}y\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{I}}|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}|^{2}\left|\int_{x}^{1}\varphi_{yy}^{I,1}\mathrm{d}y\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\left(\|\langle z\rangle v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.28)

Next we shall estimate
\|t\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}
. Recalling the estimates on
\|\mathcal{P}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\,(1\leq i\leq 6)
in (
4.13
)–(
4.19
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},\ \ 1\leq i\leq 6.

(4.29)

For
\mathcal{P}_{7}
, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}_{7}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\left(\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{1/2}\|t\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{1/2}+\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)\left(\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}},

(4.30)

where we have used (
3.12
), (
3.55
), (
3.56
), (
3.57
), (
3.61
) and (
A.2
). By analogous arguments as in deriving (
4.30
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, one can modify the estimates (
4.20
)–(
4.21
) to get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{P}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\ \ \mbox{for }i=8,9,10,\ \ \ \|t\mathcal{P}_{11}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(4.31)

where we have used the condition
0<\varepsilon<1
. Now let us turn to estimate for
\|t^{\frac{9}{8}}F\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}
. Similar to the derivation of (
4.22
)–(
4.24
), we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-\nu},

(4.32a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon\|t\varphi_{t}^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\|_{L^{2}}+C\varepsilon\|t\varphi_{t}^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1+\nu}\left[\|t^{2}\varphi_{t}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{t}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|t^{2}\varphi_{t}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{t}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2+\nu-\alpha}{2}},

(4.32b)

where we have used (
3.59
) and (
3.62
). In virtue of (
3.59
), (
3.62
), (
4.25
), (
4.32
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, we proceed to modify the estimate (
4.26
) as

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|tF^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\leq C\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\left(1+\left\|\varphi^{I,0}\right\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad~\ \ \quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\ \ \ \quad\qquad\qquad\left.+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}+C\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime\prime}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\|t\partial_{t}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}-\nu}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2-3\nu}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2+\nu-\alpha}{2}}.

(4.33)

Gathering (
4.29
), (
4.30
), (
4.31
) and (
4.33
), we then arrive at

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}},

where
\iota_{0}
is as in (
2.26
). For
\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}
, by (
A.2
), Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
and analogous arguments as in (
4.28
), we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\left(\|\langle z\rangle v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now we proceed to estimate
\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
and
\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
. Notice that if
\|h\chi\|_{Z}\leq C\|h\|_{X}\|\chi\|_{Y}
for
h\in X
and
\chi\in Y
with
X
,
Y
and
Z
being Banach spaces, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}(h\chi)\|_{Z}\leq C\|\partial_{t}h\|_{X}\|\chi\|_{Y}+C\|h\|_{X}\|\partial_{t}\chi\|_{Y},

(4.34)

provided
\partial_{t}f\in X
and
\partial_{t}\chi\in Y
, and that the time-weighted estimates (e.g., the weight function
t^{\beta}
with
\beta\leq 3
) of the profiles are uniform with respect to
\varepsilon
. Therefore, by (
3.7
), (
A.6a
) and (
3.11
) and similar arguments as proving (
4.13
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{P}_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\partial_{t}\partial_{x}\varphi^{I,0}(x,t)-\partial_{t}\partial_{x}\varphi^{I,0}(0,t)-x\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}\varphi^{I,0}(0,t)}{x^{2}}x^{2}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\frac{\partial_{t}\partial_{x}\varphi^{I,0}(x,t)-\partial_{t}\partial_{x}\varphi^{I,0}(0,t)-x\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{2}\varphi^{I,0}(0,t)}{x^{2}}x^{2}\partial_{t}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\right\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{t}\partial_{x}^{3}\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|x^{2}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{x}^{3}\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|x^{2}\partial_{t}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{t}\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{4}}\|z^{2}v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{4}}\|z^{2}\partial_{t}v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}.

(4.35)

Similar arguments further entails that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{P}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}}\ \ \mbox{for }i=2,3,\cdots,11,\ \ \ \|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},

(4.36)

where
\iota_{0}
is as in (
4.27
). Now it remains to prove
\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}F\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{3/4-\nu}
. For this, we first derive some more estimates on
b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}
and
b_{v}^{\varepsilon}
. Indeed, by analogous arguments as in (
4.22
)–(
4.24
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon\|\varphi_{t}^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon\|t\varphi_{t}^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-\nu},

(4.37a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\|x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1+\frac{\nu}{2}},

(4.37b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\left(\|\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\|\partial_{t}\Lambda_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(4.37c)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\left(\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}\Lambda_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\alpha},

(4.37d)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}^{2}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\left(\|t\partial_{t}^{2}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\|t\partial_{t}^{2}\Lambda_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\right)\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(4.37e)

where we have used (
3.57
), (
3.1
) and (
3.2
). Notice also that
\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}
is independent of
x
. Thus it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}+\|\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

(4.38)

With (
3.7
), (
4.32
), (
4.37
), (
4.38
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, recalling the definition of
F^{\varepsilon}
in (
4.11
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}F^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}((0,T))}\left(1+\|\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\ \left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad~\ \ \quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\left(1+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad~\ \ \quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\varphi_{t}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v_{t}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{t}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{t}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\ \ \ \quad\qquad\qquad\left.+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{zt}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{\xi t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right.

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\ \ \ \quad\qquad\qquad\left.+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}-\nu},

where we have used
0<\varepsilon<1
and
\alpha>1
. This along with (
4.9
), (
4.35
) and (
4.36
) gives
\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}}
, and thus the proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 4.2
.

Let
0<T<\infty
,
0<\varepsilon<1
and
1<\alpha<1+\nu<5/4
. Then there exists a constant
C>0
independent of
\varepsilon
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{1}},\ \ \|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{2}},

(4.39)

where
\iota_{1}=\min\left\{1,\frac{\alpha}{2},\frac{3-\alpha}{2},1-\nu\right\}\geq\iota_{0}
,
\iota_{3}=\min\left\{1,\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2},\frac{3-\alpha}{2},\frac{\alpha}{2}\right\}\geq\iota_{0}
, the constant
C>0
is independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

From (
2.11
)–(
2.17
), we know that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle v_{zz}^{B,\varepsilon}=v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}+(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)v^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}(v^{B,\varepsilon}+v^{I,0}(0,t)),\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v_{\xi\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}=v_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}+(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)+M)v^{b,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{z}^{b,\varepsilon}(v^{b,\varepsilon}+v^{I,0}(1,t)).\end{cases}

(4.40)

Plugging (
4.40
) into
\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}
in (
4.7
), recalling the definition of
\Phi^{a}
and
V^{a}
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=-\left[v^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)-x\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}(0,t))+v^{b,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)-(x-1)\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[v^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(0,t))+v^{b,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[v^{B,1}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t))+v^{b,1}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}(v^{I,0}-v^{I,0}(0,t)-xv_{x}^{I,0}(0,t))+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\left(v^{I,0}-v^{I,0}(1,t)-(x-1)v_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon\left[\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}(v^{I,1}(x,t)-v^{I,1}(0,t))+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}(v^{I,1}(x,t)-v^{I,1}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon\left[\varphi_{x}^{B,2}(v^{I,0}(x,t)-v^{I,0}(0,t))+\varphi_{x}^{b,2}(v^{I,0}(x,t)-v^{I,0}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1})-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}v^{b,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}v^{B,\varepsilon}\right)-\varepsilon\left(\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}v^{b,1}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}v^{B,1}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon\left(\varphi_{x}^{B,2}v^{b,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,2}v^{B,\varepsilon}\right)-\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{B,2}(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1})-\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{b,2}(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-b_{v}^{\varepsilon}[\varphi_{x}^{I,0}+M+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}+\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon})+\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}(v^{I,0}+v^{B,\varepsilon}+v^{b,\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(v^{I,1}+v^{B,1}+v^{b,1}))+\left[\varepsilon v_{xx}^{I,0}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{xx}^{I,1}\right]-\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}=:\sum_{i=1}^{16}\mathcal{K}_{i}.

(4.41)

The proof of (
4.39
) is quite similar as the one for Lemma
4.1
. We first prove
\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{3/4}
. By (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
A.6
) and Taylor’s formula, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\partial_{x}^{3}\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|\langle z\rangle^{2}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle^{2}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}.

Similarly, by (
3.8
), (
3.11
), (
3.12
), (
3.57
), (
A.4
) and (
A.6b
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{3}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\left(\|\langle z\rangle v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{4}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\left(\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\|v_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{6}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\left(\|\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\|v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}.\end{cases}

For
\mathcal{K}_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{I}}|v^{B,\varepsilon}|^{2}\Big(\int_{0}^{x}\varphi_{yy}^{I,1}\mathrm{d}y\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathcal{I}}|v^{b,\varepsilon}|^{2}\Big(\int_{1}^{x}\varphi_{yy}^{I,1}\mathrm{d}y\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\left(\|\langle z\rangle v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq\varepsilon,

where we have used (
3.11
), (
3.45
) and (
3.47
). Similarly, we get for
\mathcal{K}_{5}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{5}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon\left(\|\langle z\rangle\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon.

In view of (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
3.60
) and (
A.2
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{7}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\left(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon.

(4.42)

By analogous arguments as in (
4.42
) along with (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
4.22
), (
4.25
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, we derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{11}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\left(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}},

(4.43a)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{12}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\left(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}},

(4.43b)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{13}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\left(1+\|\varphi_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad~\qquad\qquad\left.+\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\right)\|b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(4.43c)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{14}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\left(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2-\nu}{2}}.

(4.43d)

Recalling the arguments in proving the estimate on
\mathcal{P}_{5}
in (
4.19
), we get, thanks to (
4.14
)–(
4.15
) and Lemmas
3.3
–
3.6
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}_{8}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{K}_{9}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{K}_{10}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}}.

For
\mathcal{K}_{15}
and
\mathcal{K}_{16}
, it follows from (
3.7
), (
3.47
) and (
4.37c
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{15}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq\varepsilon\|v_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3-\alpha}{2}},\ \ \ \|\mathcal{K}_{16}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

Substituting the estimates
\|\mathcal{K}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
into (
4.41
), we then get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{1}},

where
\iota_{1}=\min\left\{1,\frac{\alpha}{2},\frac{3-\alpha}{2},1-\nu\right\}\geq\iota_{0}
for
1<\alpha<5/4
. Next we estimate
\|t^{1/2}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}
. By repeating the above arguments on
\mathcal{K}_{i}
with
L_{T}^{2}
replaced by
L_{T}^{\infty}
, one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}},\ \ \mbox{for }i=1,3,4,7,8,9,10,\ \ \|\mathcal{K}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\ \mbox{for }i=6,11,12,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{5}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon,\ \ \|\mathcal{K}_{15}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3-\alpha}{2}},

where (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
4.22
), (
4.25
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
have been used. For
\mathcal{K}_{2}
, by virtue of (
3.4
), (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
3.55
) and (
A.2
), it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}_{2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{5/4}\left(\|\langle z\rangle v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle\xi\rangle v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

Furthermore, thanks to (
4.25
) and (
4.32a
), we can modify (
4.43c
) to derive

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{K}_{13}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\|\varphi_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)\|b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{K}_{14}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\left(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}})\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

where we have used the condition
\nu<1/4<1
. To estimate
\|t^{1/2}\mathcal{K}_{16}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}
, we need to derive a more delicate estimate for
\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}
. Indeed, by analogous arguments as in (
4.25
), it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+C\left(\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}\Lambda_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\right)\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(4.44)

where we have used (
3.2
). Therefore we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{K}_{16}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

Gathering the estimates on the
