\|\cdot\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}
norms of
\mathcal{K}_{i}
, we thus conclude that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{2}},

where
\iota_{2}=\min\left\{1,\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\alpha}{2},\frac{3-\alpha}{2},\frac{\alpha}{2}\right\}\geq\iota_{0}
for
1<\alpha<5/4
. In what follows, we will estimate
\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
. By repeating the arguments for estimates on
\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
, in view of (
4.34
) and the fact that the time-weighted norms of the profiles involved in
\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\,(1\leq i\leq 12)
are uniform with respect to
\varepsilon
, says

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\varphi_{txx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|t\varphi_{zt}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|t\varphi_{\xi t}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}+\|tv_{tx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C

for some constant
C>0
independent of
\varepsilon
, one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{1}}\ \ \mbox{for }1\leq i\leq 12.

(4.45)

It now remains to estimate
\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{i}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
for
i=13,14,15,16
. It follows from (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
4.22
), (
4.24
), (
4.25
), (
4.37
), (
4.44
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{13}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\|t\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(\|\varphi_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|\varphi_{xt}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t\varphi_{xt}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|t\varphi_{zt}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}\|t\varphi_{\xi t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|t\partial_{t}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+\|b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|t\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{14}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|t\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}})\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\left(1+\|t\partial_{t}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|t\partial_{t}v^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\left.\qquad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|t\partial_{t}v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|t\partial_{t}v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|t\partial_{t}v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}})\right)\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

For
\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{15}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}
, due to (
3.7
) and (
3.47d
), it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{15}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon\|v_{txx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v_{txx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3-\alpha}{2}}

for
1<\alpha<5/4
and
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. Finally, we utilize (
4.37e
) to derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{K}_{16}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}=\|t\partial_{t}^{2}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

(4.46)

Therefore we summarize from (
4.45
)–(
4.46
) that
\|t\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{1}}
. The proof of Lemma
4.2
is complete.
∎

To derive some uniform-in-
\varepsilon
estimates for
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
, we need to introduce some preparatory results. Denote
\hat{\Phi}=\varphi_{x}^{I,0}+\varepsilon^{1/2}(\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon})
. In view of (
2.8
) and (
2.10
), it holds for any
T>0
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle 0<K_{\ast}^{-1}\leq\hat{\Phi}+M\leq K_{\ast}

(4.47)

for some constant
K_{\ast}>0
independent of
\varepsilon
, provided
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{1}
for some small constant
\varepsilon_{1}>0
which may depend on
T
. Furthermore, from (
2.15
) and
\eqref{first-bd-pro-rt-approxi}_{2}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}_{zz}-\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}_{z}v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}=(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon},\ \ \varphi^{b,\varepsilon}_{\xi\xi}-\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}_{\xi}v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}=(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)+M)v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}.

This along with
\eqref{eq-outer-0}_{1}
and (
4.2
) implies that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}

\displaystyle=-\left[v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t))+v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\left[v_{x}^{I,0}+v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{x}^{I,1}+v_{x}^{B,1}+v_{x}^{b,1})+\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\left[v_{x}^{I,0}+v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{x}^{I,1}+v_{x}^{B,1}+v_{x}^{b,1})+\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}+M)\left[\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(v_{x}^{I,1}+v_{x}^{B,1}+v_{x}^{b,1})+\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\right]+\varphi_{t}^{I,0}.

We proceed to show that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\leq C.

(4.48)

Indeed, thanks to (
3.7
), (
3.8
), (
3.12
), (
3.9a
), (
3.10a
) and (
3.46a
), we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\leq\|\varphi_{xt}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{zt}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{\xi t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\leq C

(4.49)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\left(\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)+C\left(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\left(\|v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\times(1+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}})+\|\varphi_{t}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\leq C,

where we have used

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|v_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C

for
1<\alpha<5/4
, and the following inequality

\displaystyle\displaystyle C\left(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon

due to (
4.14
), (
4.15
) and similar arguments as in (
4.17
). Furthermore, recall the definition of
\Phi^{a}
and
\hat{\Phi}
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Phi_{x}^{a}+M=\hat{\Phi}+M+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{I,1}+\varepsilon\left(\varphi_{x}^{B,2}+\varphi_{x}^{b,2}\right)+\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}=:\hat{\Phi}+M+\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}},

(4.50)

with
\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}=\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{I,1}+\varepsilon(\varphi_{x}^{B,2}+\varphi_{x}^{b,2})+\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}
satisfying

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}-\nu})\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},

(4.51)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}^{\text{err}}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{zz}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi_{\xi\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}^{2}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}+C+C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-2\nu}\leq C

for
1<\alpha<1+\nu<5/4
, where we have used (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
3.60
), (
4.23
), (
A.2
) and analogous arguments as in (
4.23
). Finally, we introduce some time-weighted estimates on the approximate solutions
(\Phi^{a},V^{a})
.

Lemma 4.3
.

Let
0<\varepsilon<1
. It holds for any
T>0
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}V_{x}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}V_{t}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}V_{tx}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|t^{\frac{9}{4}}\Phi_{xt}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C,

(4.52)

where
C>0
is a constant independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

From (
3.8
), (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
3.56
), (
3.57
), (
3.62
), (
4.25
) and (
A.6
), we get

\displaystyle\|tV_{x}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|tv_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|tv_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|tv_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\left(\|tv_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\right)+\|t\partial_{x}b_{v}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}},

where
C>0
is a constant that may depend on
T
but independent of
\varepsilon
. By (
3.8
), (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
3.56
), (
3.57
), (
3.62
), (
4.2
), (
4.44
) and (
A.6
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}V_{t}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\left(\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}b_{v}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}\leq C.

Notice that
\partial_{x}b_{v}
is independent of
x
. Then we have from (
4.44
) that
\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{v}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C
. Hence, it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}V_{tx}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{tx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{tz}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|t^{3}v_{tx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{txx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{tx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\left(\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{tz}^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}v_{t\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\right)+\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{v}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}},

(4.53)

where we have used (
3.8
), (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
3.56
), (
3.57
), (
3.62
), (
4.2
) and (
A.6
). By analogous arguments as proving (
4.37b
), we know that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{2}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+\varepsilon\|t^{2}\varphi_{t}^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|t^{2}\varphi_{t}^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-\nu}\leq C.

This along with analogous arguments as proving (
4.53
) implies that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{2}\Phi_{xt}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|t^{2}\varphi_{tx}^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\left(\|t^{2}\varphi_{tz}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{1}}+\|t^{2}\varphi_{t\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{1}}\right)+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{2}\varphi_{tx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|t^{2}\varphi_{tz}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|t^{2}\varphi_{t\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\right)+\|t^{2}\partial_{t}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C

for some constant
C>0
independent of
\varepsilon
. Similar arguments along with (
4.32a
) further yields that
\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C
. The proof is complete.
∎

4.2.2.
Lower-order estimates

Now we shall establish some uniform-in-
\varepsilon
estimates for
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
by beginning with the
L^{2}
estimates. Throughout this section, we assume that
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
satisfies for any
T>0
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\right)\leq\delta,

(4.54)

for some small constant
\delta>0
to be determined later. We remark that
\delta
could depend on
T
here since we are mainly concerned with the zero-diffusion limit of the problem with
T>0
fixed. Besides, our estimates in the sequel are based on the estimates established in previous sections. Thus we always assume the conditions in Lemmas
3.2
–
3.7
,
4.1
,
4.2
hold, and the results therein will be frequently used in the subsequent analysis without further clarification.

Lemma 4.4
.

In addition to the conditions in Proposition
4.1
, we assume that the solution
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
to (
4.6
) on
[0,T]
satisfies (
4.54
). Then there exists a constant
\delta_{1}>0
such that for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\|V^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},

(4.55)

provided
\delta\leq\delta_{1}
, where
\iota_{0}
is as in (
2.26
), and
C>0
is a constant depending on
T
but independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

Multiplying the first equation in (
4.6
) by
\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}
followed by an integration over
\mathcal{I}
and integration by parts, one deduces that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}\hat{\Phi}_{t}\mathrm{d}x-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi_{x}^{a}+M}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}\left(\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\right)\mathrm{d}x.

(4.56)

The terms on the right hand side of (
4.56
) can be treated as follows. By (
4.47
), we derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}\hat{\Phi}_{t}\mathrm{d}x\leq C\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x.

(4.57)

Thanks to (
4.47
), (
4.54
), (
A.3
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{32}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C(\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{32}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\delta\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x\leq\frac{1}{16}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x,

(4.58)

provided
C\delta\leq\frac{1}{32}
. Recalling (
4.8
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x=\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x,

(4.59)

where, due to (
4.47
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x\leq\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.60)

For the second term on the right hand side of (
4.59
), by (
4.10
) and integration by parts, we first split it into three terms:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{I}}v_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(0,t))\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}v_{x}^{b,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(1,t))\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}v_{x}^{I,1}(\varphi_{x}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{x}^{b,\varepsilon})\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x=:\mathscr{D}_{1}+\mathscr{D}_{2}+\mathscr{D}_{3}.

(4.61)

Next we will estimate
\mathscr{D}_{i}\,(i=1,2,3)
term by term. For
\mathscr{D}_{1}
, we get by virtue of (
4.47
), (
A.3
), (
A.6a
), Corollaries
3.1
–
3.3
, integration by parts and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{I}}v^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(0,t))\frac{\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}v^{B,\varepsilon}\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\Phi^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\mathcal{I}}v^{B,\varepsilon}(\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(x,t)-\varphi_{x}^{I,1}(0,t))\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}(\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\varphi_{xx}^{B,\varepsilon}+\varphi_{xx}^{b,\varepsilon}))\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\|\Phi_{x}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{I}}|v^{B,\varepsilon}|^{2}\left|\int_{0}^{x}\varphi_{yy}^{I,1}\mathrm{d}y\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{I}}|v^{B,\varepsilon}|^{2}\left|\int_{0}^{x}\varphi_{yy}^{I,1}\mathrm{d}y\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\varphi_{xx}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\varphi_{xx}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}})\right)

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{24}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\langle z\rangle v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{2}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(1+\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\varphi_{zz}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{2}+\|\varphi_{\xi\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{24}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

In the same method, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{24}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
\mathscr{D}_{3}
, it follows from (
3.12
), (
3.56
), (
4.47
), (
A.4
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{3}\leq C\|v_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|\varphi_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\varphi_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{1}{24}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore we have from (
4.61
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

This along with (
4.60
) gives

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.62)

Recalling (
4.50
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi_{x}^{a}+M}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi^{\varepsilon}\frac{\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x=:\mathcal{L}_{1}+\mathcal{L}_{2},

(4.63)

where, thanks to (
4.47
), integration by parts and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}=\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x\leq\frac{1}{16}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{L}_{2}
, we get by (
4.47
), (
4.54
), (
A.6a
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{32}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Therefore we have from (
4.63
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi_{x}^{a}+M}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{16}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\varepsilon}{32}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.64)

For the last term in (
4.56
), from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}\left(\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\right)\mathrm{d}x\leq C\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\leq\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x.

(4.65)

Thus, plugging (
4.57
), (
4.58
), (
4.62
), (
4.64
) and (
4.65
) into (
4.56
), it follows that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{32}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1\right)\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.66)

provided
C\delta\leq\frac{1}{32}
in (
4.58
), where we have used (
4.47
).

To proceed, multiplying the second equation in (
4.6
) by
V^{\varepsilon}
and integrating the resulting equation over
\mathcal{I}
, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}|V^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{a}V^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)\left|V^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x=\sum_{i=1}^{4}\mathcal{N}_{i},

(4.67)

where, due to integration by parts and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{1}

\displaystyle=2\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\Phi^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x.

(4.68)

For
\mathcal{N}_{2}
, we first notice from (
4.25
) and Corollaries
3.1
–
3.6
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|V^{a}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C

(4.69)

for some constant
C>0
independent of
\varepsilon
. This along with (
4.47
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality yields that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|V^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{N}_{3}
, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, one has
\mathcal{N}_{3}\leq\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
. With (
4.50
),
\mathcal{N}_{4}
can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{4}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{I}}(\hat{\Phi}+M)|V^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}|V^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x\leq-\int_{\mathcal{I}}(\hat{\Phi}+M)|V^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Inserting the estimates on
\mathcal{N}_{i}~(1\leq i\leq 4)
into (
4.67
), we get by virtue of (
4.47
) and Lemma
4.2
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{7\varepsilon}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}(\hat{\Phi}+M)\left|V^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+C(\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.70)

Combining (
4.70
) with (
4.66
), in view of (
4.47
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left(|V^{\varepsilon}|^{2}+\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\right)\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left(\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}+\varepsilon|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}+(\hat{\Phi}+M)\left|V^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\left(1+\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}(\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\left(\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1\right)\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x,

which along with (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
3.60
), (
4.22
), (
4.23
), (
4.48
), (
4.51
), (
A.2
), (
A.4
), Lemmas
4.1
,
4.2
and the Gronwall inequality entails for any
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|V^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+\|\varphi_{xx}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\varepsilon^{-1}(\|\mathcal{R}_{1,1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2})\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},

where we have used
\iota_{1}\geq\iota_{0}
and
\frac{1}{2}<\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
. The proof of Lemma
4.4
is complete.
∎

We proceed to establish
H^{1}
estimate for
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
.

Lemma 4.5
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
4.4
, it holds for any
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|V_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},\ \ \ \|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}},

(4.71)

provided
\delta
is suitably small, where the constant
C>0
is independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

Multiplying the second equation in (
4.6
) by
V_{t}^{\varepsilon}
and integrating the resulting equation over
\mathcal{I}
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon\left|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{I}}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)V^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{a}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x,

(4.72)

where, due to (
4.47
), (
4.50
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{I}}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)V^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x\leq\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\Big(1+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\Big)\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.73)

where
\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}
is as in (
4.50
). By (
4.69
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{I}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{a}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x\leq\|V^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.74)

Thanks to (
4.55
), (
A.3
), the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Lemma
4.2
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.75)

and that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.76)

With (
4.73
)–(
4.76
) and the fact
0<\varepsilon<1
, we thus update (
4.72
) as

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon\left|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(1+\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\Big(1+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\Big)\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which followed by an integration over
[0,t]
for any
t\in(0,T]
gives

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|V_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{1}-1},

(4.77)

where we have used (
3.47
), (
3.57
), (
3.60
), (
4.23
), (
4.39
), (
4.47
) and Lemma
4.4
. Applying the Gronwall inequality to (
4.77
), we ultimately obtain that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|V^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|V_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{1}-1}\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1},

(4.78)

where we have used the facts
\iota_{1}\geq\iota_{0}
and
0<\varepsilon<1
. Now let us turn to estimates on
\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}
. Taking the
L^{2}
inner product of the first equation in (
4.6
) with
\frac{\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}
followed by an integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{(\hat{\Phi}+M)^{2}}\hat{\Phi}_{t}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\left(\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\right)\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\Phi_{x}^{a}+M}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}}{\hat{\Phi}_{x}+M}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x=:\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{Q}_{i}.

(4.79)

We next estimate
\mathcal{Q}_{i}~(1\leq i\leq 5)
. Thanks to (
4.47
), we get
\mathcal{Q}_{1}\leq C\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
. From(
4.47
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we deduce that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}||\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}|}{\hat{\Phi}+M}|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-\hat{\Phi}V_{x}^{a}|\mathrm{d}x\leq C\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-\hat{\Phi}V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-\hat{\Phi}V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By (
3.8
), (
3.11
), (
3.45
), (
3.56
), (
3.57
), (
3.62
), (
4.25
), (
4.47
), (
4.50
), (
4.78
), (
A.2
) and the first equation in (
4.6
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|V_{x}^{a}-v_{x}^{I,0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(1+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{z}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|v_{\xi}^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|v_{z}^{B,1}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{2}+\|v_{\xi}^{b,1}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}^{2})+\|\partial_{x}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(1+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(1+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})

for
0<\varepsilon<1
. Therefore we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}})+C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(1+\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}).

(4.80)

With (
4.47
), (
4.54
), (
4.78
), (
4.80
), (
A.2
), the condition
0<\varepsilon<1
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, one can estimate
\mathcal{Q}_{3}
as follows:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}})\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}})\left(\|\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}})\Big(\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)+\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}})\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}})\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{1}{16}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{1}{16}+C\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}(\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}})\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{3\iota_{0}-2}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}(\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2},

(4.81)

provided
C\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{16}
. For
\mathcal{Q}_{4}
, we utilize (
4.50
), (
4.78
), integration by parts and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{4}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\frac{\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}}{\hat{\Phi}+M}\mathrm{d}x\leq\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{8}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{8}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2(\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-2}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Finally, by the Cauchy-Schwarz equality, we have for
\mathcal{Q}_{5}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{5}\leq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Therefore, inserting estimates on
\mathcal{Q}_{i}~(0\leq i\leq 5)
into (
4.79
), we get after taking
\varepsilon
suitably small that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}_{x}+M}\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}\frac{\left|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}}{\hat{\Phi}_{x}+M}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+C\left(\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{4\iota_{0}-3}{2}}(\varepsilon\|V_{x}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C(\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{-1})\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

Integrating the above inequality over
(0,t)
for any
t\in[0,T]
, we derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathcal{I}}V^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\hat{\Phi}_{\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{\frac{4\iota_{0}-3}{2}}(\varepsilon\|V_{x}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2})+C(\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{-1})\|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}}\|\hat{\Phi}^{\text{err}}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|V^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\hat{\Phi}_{\tau}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}}+C

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\hat{\Phi}_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\hat{\Phi}_{x}-(\hat{\Phi}+M)V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}},

where we have used (
4.12
) (
4.23
), (
4.51
), (
4.55
), (
4.78
),
\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
from (
2.30
),
0<\varepsilon<1
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Therefore by (
4.48
) and the Gronwall inequality, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}+2\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2}}.

This along with (
4.78
) gives (
4.71
). Thus we finish the proof of Lemma
4.5
.
∎

As a direct consequence of the Lemmas
4.4
and
4.5
, we have the following corollary.

Corollary 4.1
.

Assume the conditions of Lemmas
4.4
and
4.5
hold. Then for any solution
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
to (
4.6
) on
[0,T]
satisfying (
4.54
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}-\iota_{0}}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\mathrm{d}t\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}.

(4.82)

where
C>0
is a constant depending on
T
but independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

From (
4.55
), (
4.71
), (
A.2
), (
A.3
),
0<\varepsilon<1
and the fact
\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{2\iota_{0}-2}{2}+\frac{2\iota_{0}-1}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}},

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}(4\iota_{0}-\frac{5}{2})+\frac{2\iota_{0}-1}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}\leq C\varepsilon^{3\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{4}}

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}

for any
t\in[0,T]
. The proof is complete.
∎

Remark 4.1
.

In view of (
4.71
) and (
4.82
), the
a priori
assumption (
4.54
) can be verified. Indeed, the estimates (
4.71
) and (
4.82
) hold provided
\delta
is suitably small, says
\delta\leq\delta_{0}
for some constant
\delta_{0}<1
(see (
4.58
) and (
4.81
)). Therefore if we fix
\delta=\frac{\delta_{0}}{2}
, then we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{4}}<\delta,

provided
\varepsilon>0
is small enough. Hence, for suitably small
\varepsilon
, all the estimates in Lemmas
4.4
–
4.5
and Corollary
4.1
hold true.

4.2.3.
Higher-order estimates

To finish the proof of Proposition
4.1
, we need to derive some higher-order estimates for
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
in this subsection.

Lemma 4.6
.

Under the conditions of Lemmas
4.4
and
4.5
, the solution
(\Phi^{\varepsilon},V^{\varepsilon})
to (
4.6
) on
[0,T]
possesses the following estimate:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xx}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}},\ \ \ t\in(0,T],

where
C>0
is a constant independent of
\varepsilon
.

Proof.

Differentiating the equations in (
4.6
) with respect to
t
, then it follows that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\makebox[-4.7pt]{~}\begin{cases}\displaystyle\Phi_{tt}^{\varepsilon}=\Phi_{xxt}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}-\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{a}-\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{xt}^{a}-V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)-V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{xt}^{a}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon},\\[2.84526pt]
\displaystyle V_{tt}^{\varepsilon}=\varepsilon V_{xxt}^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V^{\varepsilon}-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}-\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V^{a}-\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{a}-\Phi_{xt}^{a}V^{\varepsilon}-(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)V_{t}^{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}.\end{cases}\makebox[-5.0pt]{~}

(4.83)

Multiplying the first equation in (
4.83
) by
t^{5}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}
and then integrating the resulting equation over
\mathcal{I}
, we get after using integration by parts that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\left|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\left|\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=\frac{5}{2}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{4}\left|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V_{x}^{a}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{xt}^{a}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{xt}^{a}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x=:\sum_{i=1}^{8}\mathcal{H}_{i}.

(4.84)

Next we estimate
\mathcal{H}_{i}\,(1\leq i\leq 7)
term by term. Clearly, we have
\mathcal{H}_{1}\leq C\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
. In view of (
4.71
), (
A.3
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq(\frac{1}{8}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1})\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.85)

Similarly, by (
4.71
), (
A.2
),
0<\varepsilon<1
, the fact
\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}<\frac{5}{8}
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{16}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{16}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+Ct^{\frac{5}{2}}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi_{tx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{16}(\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(1+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{16}(\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+C\varepsilon^{8\iota_{0}-5}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{H}_{i}~(4\leq i\leq 7)
, thanks to (
4.52
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{4}\leq\frac{1}{8}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{x}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{5}\leq C\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{6}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{16}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{16}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{7}\leq C\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-\iota_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-\iota_{0}+\frac{1}{4}}\|\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{8}\leq\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.\end{cases}

(4.86)

Plugging estimates on
\mathcal{H}_{i}\,(1\leq i\leq 8)
into (
4.84
) followed by an integration over
(0,t)
for any
t\in(0,T]
, it follows that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\|\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{5}\|V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C(1+\varepsilon^{8\iota_{0}-5}+\varepsilon^{-1}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{-\iota_{0}+\frac{1}{4}})\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+\int_{0}^{t}\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}\|V_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{5}\|V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{3\iota_{0}-\frac{9}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{5}\|V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}},

(4.87)

provided
C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}\leq\frac{1}{8}
in (
4.85
),
where we have used
\frac{1}{2}<\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
,
0<\varepsilon<1
and Lemmas
4.1
,
4.4
and
4.5
. To proceed, multiplying the second equation in (
4.83
) by
t^{5}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}
, we get after integrating the resulting equation over
\mathcal{I}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\left|V_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}t^{5}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}V^{a}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{a}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\Phi_{xt}^{a}V^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathcal{I}}t^{5}\left(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M\right)\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=:C\int_{\mathcal{I}}\left|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\right|^{2}\mathrm{d}x+\sum_{i=1}^{7}\mathcal{L}_{i},

(4.88)

where, similar to (
4.86
),
\mathcal{L}_{i}\,(1\leq i\leq 6)
enjoy the following estimates:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\ \ \ \mathcal{L}_{2}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{8}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{tx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{3}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|V^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\ \ \ \mathcal{L}_{4}\leq\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{t}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{5}\leq C\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xt}^{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),\ \ \ \mathcal{L}_{6}\leq\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{7}\leq C\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|V_{t}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.\end{cases}

(4.89)

Therefore, we integrate (
4.88
) over
(0,t)\subset(0,T]
to get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\int_{0}^{t}\left(1+\varepsilon\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}(\Phi_{x}^{a}+M)\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|V_{\tau}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+C\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+C\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{-1}\|t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C(1+\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{3\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{4}})\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}}.

(4.90)

where we have used (
4.52
), (
4.55
), (
4.71
), (
4.82
),
0<\varepsilon<1
,
\iota_{2}\geq\iota_{0}
,
\frac{1}{2}<\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
and Lemma
4.2
. Combining (
4.90
) with (
4.87
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{t}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{t}^{\varepsilon}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\tau^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{x\tau}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{4\iota_{0}-\frac{7}{2}}.

This along with (
4.6
),(
4.52
), (
4.55
), (
4.71
) and Lemmas
4.1
,
4.2
further entails that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}-4\iota_{0}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{9}{2}-4\iota_{0}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}V_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}\leq C.

Consequently, we finish the proof of Lemma
4.6
.
∎

4.3.
Proof of Theorem
2.1

Based on Proposition
4.1
, we prove the estimates of
\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}
and
\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\varepsilon}
asserted in (
4.1
) and further show the convergence results stated in Theorem
2.1
. Recalling from (
4.25
) and Corollaries
3.2
–
3.6
, we get for
1<\alpha<5/4
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{4}}\left(\|\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)+\|\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C,

(4.91a)

\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{z}^{2}}+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}H_{\xi}^{2}}+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{x}^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C,

(4.91b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|v^{B,1}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L_{z}^{\infty})}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L_{\xi}^{\infty})}+\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{l}b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C,

(4.91c)

where
l=0,1
. By analogous arguments as proving (
4.23
), we get by virtue of (
4.15
), (
4.16
), (
3.59
) and (
3.62
) that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+\varepsilon\|\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-\frac{\alpha}{4}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{8}},

(4.92)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{B,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[(1-x){\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{b,2}(0,t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T))}\|[x{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{-\frac{1-x}{\varepsilon^{\nu}}}]^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon+C\varepsilon^{1-\nu}\leq C\varepsilon^{1-\nu},

(4.93)

where we have used
1<\alpha<5/4
. Therefore, we get from (
4.5
), (
4.82
), (
4.91
), (
4.92
),
1<\alpha<1+\nu<5/4
and
\frac{1}{2}<\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\left(\|\varphi^{\varepsilon B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi^{\varepsilon b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)+\|b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{1-\frac{\alpha}{4}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{8}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3\iota_{0}}{2}-\frac{3}{8}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3\iota_{0}}{2}-\frac{3}{8}}

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\varphi^{\varepsilon}-\varphi^{I,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\varphi^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)+C\|\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{3\iota_{0}}{2}-\frac{3}{8}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{3\iota_{0}}{2}-\frac{3}{8}},

which gives (
2.27
). Furthermore,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|v^{I,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\|v^{B,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|v^{b,1}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+C\|b_{v}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|V^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\iota_{0}-\frac{1}{4}}.

(4.94)

This yields (
2.28
). To prove (
2.29
),
which is associated with the estimate on
\partial_{x}\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}
, we first utilize (
A.3
), Lemmas
4.5
and
4.6
to derive that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{4}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\left(\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{5}{2}}\Phi_{xx}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.95)

Then by (
4.5
), (
4.91b
), (
4.93
), (
4.95
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|t^{\frac{5}{4}}\partial_{x}\mathcal{E}_{1}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{z}^{B,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{\infty}}+\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\varphi_{\xi}^{b,2}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\right)+C\|t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}b_{\varphi}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|t^{\frac{5}{4}}\Phi_{x}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{2\iota_{0}-1}

for
\frac{1}{2}<\iota_{0}<\frac{7}{12}
. This gives (
2.29
) and completes the proof of Theorem
2.1
.
\square

Appendix A
Some basic facts

To make our work self-contained, below we will list some frequently used Sobolev-type inequalities and an embedding theorem on space-time Sobolev spaces (see also the Appendix of
[
5
]
).

Lemma A.1

(
[
3
, Page 236]
)
.

Let
p>1
. Then for any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a positive constant
C=C(\epsilon,p)
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|h\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\epsilon\|h_{x}\|_{L^{p}(\mathcal{I})}+C\|h\|_{L^{1}(\mathcal{I})}

(A.1)

for any
h\in W^{1,p}(\mathcal{I})
.

Lemma A.2
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|h\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\left(\|h\|_{L^{2}}+\|h\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\|h_{x}\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\right)

(A.2)

for any
h\in H^{1}(\mathcal{I})
, where
C>0
is a positive constant independent of
h
.

We remark that if
h\in H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{I})
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|h\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\sqrt{2}\|h\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\|h_{x}\|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\ \ \mbox{and}\ \ \|h\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\|h_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{2}},

(A.3)

and that if
h\in H_{z}^{1}\,(\text{resp}.H_{\xi}^{1})
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|h\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}}\leq C\|h\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{1/2}\|h_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{1/2}\leq C\|h\|_{H_{z}^{1}}\,(\text{resp}.~\|h\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\leq C\|h\|_{L_{z}^{2}}^{1/2}\|h_{\xi}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}^{1/2}\leq C\|h\|_{H_{\xi}^{1}}),

(A.4)

where
C>0
is a constant.

The following embedding theorem is also frequently used in our analysis.

Proposition A.1

(
[
38
, Lemma 1.2]
)
.

Let
V
,
H
and
V^{\prime}
be three Hilbert spaces satisfying
V\subset H\subset V^{\prime}
with
V^{\prime}
being the dual of
V
. If a function
u
belongs to
L^{2}(0,T;V)
and its time derivatives
u_{t}
belongs to
L^{2}(0,T;V^{\prime})
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle u\in C([0,T];H)\ \mbox{ and }\ \|u\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;H)}\leq C\left(\|u\|_{L^{2}(0,T;V)}+\|u_{t}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;V^{\prime})}\right),

(A.5)

where the constant
C>0
depends on
T
but independent of
u
. In particular, we have
u\in C([0,T];H)
if
u\in L^{2}(0,T;H)
and
u_{t}\in L^{2}(0,T;H)
.

Remark A.1
.

Proposition
A.1
entails the fact that for any
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left\{u|\,u\in L^{2}(0,T;X^{m+2}),u_{t}\in L^{2}(0,T;X^{m})\right\}\hookrightarrow C([0,T];X^{m+1})

continuously, where
X^{m}:=H^{m}\,(resp.\,H_{z}^{m}\,or\,H_{\xi}^{m})
.

Finally, in view of the change of variables in (
2.4
), we have for any
G_{1}(z,t)\in H_{z}^{m}
and
G_{2}(\xi,t)\in H_{\xi}^{m}
with
m\in\mathbb{N}
that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}^{m}G_{1}\left(\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}=\varepsilon^{\frac{1-2m}{4}}\|\partial_{z}^{m}G_{1}(z,t)\|_{L_{z}^{2}},\ \ \ \left\|\partial_{x}^{m}G_{1}\left(z,t\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}=\varepsilon^{-\frac{m}{2}}\|\partial_{z}^{m}G_{1}(z,t)\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}},

(A.6a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}^{m}G_{2}\left(\frac{x-1}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}=\varepsilon^{\frac{1-2m}{4}}\|\partial_{\xi}^{m}G_{2}(\xi,t)\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}},\ \ \ \left\|\partial_{x}^{m}G_{2}\left(\xi,t\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}=\varepsilon^{-\frac{m}{2}}\|\partial_{\xi}^{m}G_{2}(\xi,t)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}.

(A.6b)

Appendix B
Approximation of the leading-order boundary-layer profiles

In this section, we shall show that
(v^{B,\varepsilon},\varphi^{B,\varepsilon})\,(\mbox{resp. }(v^{b,\varepsilon},\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}))
is a good approximation of
(v^{B,0},\varphi^{B,1})
(resp.
(v^{b,0},\varphi^{b,1})
). More precisely, we will prove (
2.14
), (
2.16
) and (
2.19
), that is,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Big\|(v^{B,\varepsilon}-v^{B,0})\Big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t\Big)\Big\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\Big\|\varepsilon^{k/2}\partial_{x}^{k}(\varphi^{B,\varepsilon}-\varphi^{B,1})\Big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t\Big)\Big\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},

(B.1)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Big\|(v^{b,\varepsilon}-v^{b,0})\Big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t\Big)\Big\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}+\Big\|\varepsilon^{k/2}\partial_{x}^{k}(\varphi^{b,\varepsilon}-\varphi^{b,1})\Big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon^{1/2}},t\Big)\Big\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

(B.2)

In what follows, we will focus only on (
B.1
). One can prove (
B.2
) similarly. Firstly, we notice from Lemma
3.3
that the problem (
2.11
) admits a unique global solution
v^{B,\varepsilon}
such that for any
l\in\mathbb{N}
and
T>0
,
\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{B,\varepsilon}\in L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{6-2k}
for
k=0,1,2,3
and

\displaystyle\displaystyle 0\leq v^{B,\varepsilon}\leq v_{\ast},\ \ \ \|\langle z\rangle^{l}v_{t}^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{3-2k}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,

(B.3a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{4-2i}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle z\rangle^{l}t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{5-2i}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }i=0,1,2,

(B.3b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}t^{2}\partial_{t}^{k}\partial_{z}^{6-2k}v^{B,\varepsilon}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,2,3.

(B.3c)

This along with the weak convergence argument and the Aubin-Lions lemma (cf. Corollary 4 of
[
34
]
) yields that the problem (
2.7
) admits a unique global solution
v^{B,0}
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle 0\leq v^{B,0}\leq v_{\ast},\ \ \ \|\langle z\rangle^{l}v_{t}^{B,0}\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L_{z}^{2}}+\|\langle z\rangle^{l}\partial_{t}^{k}v^{B,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}H_{z}^{3-2k}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }k=0,1,

(B.4a)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}t^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{k}v^{B,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }2i+k=4,\ \ \|\langle z\rangle^{l}t^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{k}v^{B,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }2i+k=5,

(B.4b)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|\langle z\rangle^{l}t^{2}\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{z}^{k}v^{B,0}\|_{L_{T}^{2}L_{z}^{2}}\leq C\ \ \mbox{for }2i+k=6.

(B.4c)

With (
B.3
) and (
B.4
), we are now ready to prove (
B.1
). Before the start, we underline that the second estimate in (
B.1
) follows directly from the first one along with (
2.8
), (
2.15
). Therefore it remains to prove the first estimate in (
B.1
). To this end, we denote

\displaystyle\displaystyle V=v^{B,\varepsilon}-v^{B,0}-\eta(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t),

with
\eta(z)
as in (
3.15
). Then, in view of (
3.1
), to prove the first estimate in (
B.1
), it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|V\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

(B.5)

From
(
2.7
)
and
(
2.11
)
, we see that
V
solves

\displaystyle\displaystyle\begin{cases}\displaystyle V_{t}=V_{zz}-(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(1,t)+M)v^{I,0}(1,t)({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,0}})-(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)\mathrm{e}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}(V+\eta(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t))\\
\displaystyle\quad\quad-(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)v^{B,0}\left({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,0}}\right)+\eta^{\prime\prime}(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)-\eta(z)\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t),\\
\displaystyle V(0,t)=0,\ \ V(+\infty,t)=0,\\
\displaystyle V(z,0)=0.\end{cases}

(B.6)

Testing the equation (
B.6
) against
V
, by the integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V^{2}\mathrm{d}z+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)\mathrm{e}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}V^{2}\mathrm{d}z+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V_{z}^{2}\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle~\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}(\varphi_{x}^{I,0}(0,t)+M)(v^{I,0}(0,t)+v^{B,0})({\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,\varepsilon}}-{\mathop{\mathrm{e}}}^{v^{B,0}})V\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\eta^{\prime\prime}(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)V\mathrm{d}z-\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\eta(z)\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)V\mathrm{d}z,

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left(|V|+|\eta(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|\right)|V|\mathrm{d}z+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\eta^{\prime\prime}(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)V\mathrm{d}z+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\eta(z)\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)V\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle~\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V^{2}\mathrm{d}z+C\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left(|\eta(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|^{2}+|\eta^{\prime\prime}(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|^{2}+|\eta(z)\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z,

(B.7)

where we have used (
3.8
), (
B.4
), (
A.2
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Integrating (
B.7
) over
(0,t)
for any
t\in(0,T]
, by (
3.7a
), (
3.1
), (
B.3
) and the fact that
\eta
is smooth and compactly supported, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V^{2}(\cdot,t)\mathrm{d}z+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left(V_{z}^{2}+V^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V^{2}\mathrm{d}z\mathrm{d}\tau+C\varepsilon^{\alpha}.

This along with the Gronwall inequality gives

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V^{2}(\cdot,t)\mathrm{d}z+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left(V_{z}^{2}+V^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{\alpha}.

(B.8)

Furthermore, testing the equation (
B.6
) against
V_{t}
, by similar arguments as deriving (
B.7
), we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V_{z}^{2}\mathrm{d}z+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V_{t}^{2}\mathrm{d}z\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}\left(|\eta(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|^{2}+|\eta^{\prime\prime}(z)\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|^{2}+|\eta(z)\partial_{t}\Lambda_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)|^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}z+C\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V^{2}\mathrm{d}z.

This along with (
3.7a
), (
3.1
), (
B.3
), (
B.8
) and the fact
\eta
is smooth and compactly supported yields that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V_{z}^{2}(\cdot,t)\mathrm{d}z+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}V_{\tau}^{2}\mathrm{d}z\mathrm{d}\tau\leq C\varepsilon^{\alpha}

(B.9)

for any
t\in[0,T]
. Combining (
B.8
), (
B.9
) and (
A.4
), we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|V\|_{L_{T}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}.

This gives (
B.5
). The proof is complete.
